Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a *New Phyrexia / Mirrodin Besieged / Scars of Mirrodin* draft from *New Phyrexia* Release Championship Top 8 Draft on [**Magic Online**](https://web.archive.org/web/20140527070414/http://www.wizards.com/Magic/Digital/MagicOnline.aspx).

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da738_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20110622000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mtgo2500501)
